\displaystyle=\left\langle{-[\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{F}\left\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle(\text{by Cauchy-Schwarz}).

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(u,v)\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\left|(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\top}}))_{u,v}\right|^{2}.

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{(u,v)\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\left\|\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\right)_{u,\cdot}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\top}})_{\cdot,v}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{v\in[k]}\left\|({\mathbf{A}^{m}})_{v,\cdot}\right\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{u\in[k]}\sum_{v\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i,u\cdot}}\left\|(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}})_{u,\cdot}\right\|^{2}_{2}.

Since
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}\in\mathcal{K}_{m}(\tau_{1},\tau_{2})
, defined in (
3.8
), by the projection operator
\mathcal{P}_{m,\tau}
in Algorithm
1
, we have

\max_{v\in[k]}\|({\mathbf{A}^{m}})_{v,\cdot}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\tau_{2}}{k_{m}}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\|^{2}_{2}
.
Furthermore, using the fact that
|\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i,u\cdot}|\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}k
, the above display can be bounded as

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tau_{2}}{k_{m}}\left\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\right\|^{2}_{2}(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}k\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}{\tau_{2}}\left\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle(k_{m}\geq k)

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}{\tau_{2}}\left\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle(\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\|_{2}\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}).

Combining the last two results, we can bound the first term in (
D.22
) as

\displaystyle\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}

\displaystyle\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}\tau_{2}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\right\|_{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\right\|_{2}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{F}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}.

(D.23)

For the second term in (
D.22
), we have

\displaystyle\left\langle{\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}},{\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\left\langle{[\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}]_{\bar{{\mathcal{S}}}_{i}}},{\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{ii}=\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{ii}^{\star}=\mathbf{I}_{k})

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{F}\left\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}]_{\bar{{\mathcal{S}}}_{i}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle(\text{by Cauchy-Schwarz}).

Let
\bar{{\mathcal{S}}}_{i,\cdot v}=\{u:(u,v)\in\bar{{\mathcal{S}}}_{i}\}
.
Note that if
(u,v)\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}
, then
\lceil{v/k}\rceil\in\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}
. Let the index set
\backslash\{i\}=\{1,\ldots,p\}\backslash\{i\}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}]_{\bar{{\mathcal{S}}}_{i}}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(u,v)\in\bar{{\mathcal{S}}}_{i}}\left|(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}|}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\})_{u,v}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{(u,v)\in\bar{{\mathcal{S}}}_{i}}\|(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}))_{u\cdot}\|_{2}^{2}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}|}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\})_{\cdot,v}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{\top}})\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\sum_{v\in[k(p-1)]}\sum_{u\in\bar{{\mathcal{S}}}_{i,\cdot v}}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}|}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\})_{\cdot,v}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{2})\alpha^{\star}k\|((\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\top}})\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}|}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}\|_{F}^{2}.

By Lemma F.9 in
Na et al. 2021
, for any two matrices
\mathbf{X}
,
\mathbf{Y}
, we have
\|\mathbf{X}\mathbf{Y}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|\mathbf{X}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{Y}\|_{1}
, where
\|\mathbf{Y}\|_{1}
denotes the maximum
\ell_{1}
-norm of the column of
\mathbf{Y}
. Then, the above display is abounded as

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{2})\alpha^{\star}k\|(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\top}})\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}\|_{1}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}|}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=(1+\vartheta_{2})\alpha^{\star}k\|\mathbf{A}^{m\top}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}\|_{1}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}|}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}\|_{F}^{2}.

Apply the condition of
\mathcal{K}_{m}(\tau_{1},\tau_{2})
, we can bound the above display as

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{2})\alpha^{\star}\tau_{2}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}\|_{1}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}|}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}\|_{F}^{2}.

Since
|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}|\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}
, and hence

\|\{\mathbf{I}_{\left|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}\right|}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}}\|\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}\|_{F}
. Along with Cauchy interlacing theorem, we can bound the above display as

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}\tau_{2}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}\|_{1}^{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}^{2}.

Finally, under assumption of Lemma
D.1
, we have

\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star\top}\|_{1}=\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star\top}\|_{1}\leq C_{B}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star\top}\|_{1}\leq C_{B}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star\top}\|_{1}
. Therefore, apply triangle inequality, we can further obtain the bound

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})(1+C_{B})^{2}s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}\tau_{2}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star\top}\|_{1}^{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}^{2},

(D.24)

Taking the results of (
D.6
)–(
D.24
) and plugging back to (
D.22
)
, we can bound
\mathcal{I}_{11,m}
with Young’s inequality and obtains

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{11,m}

\displaystyle\geq-\Upsilon_{4}\Upsilon_{3}\sqrt{s^{\star}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\geq-\Upsilon_{4}\Upsilon_{3}\sqrt{s^{\star}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{2C_{12}}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{C_{12}}{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}\right),

where we have

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{3}

\displaystyle=\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}(C_{B}+1)\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\|_{2}\max_{i\in V}(\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}\vee\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star\top}\|_{1});

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{4}

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})\tau_{2}\alpha^{\star}},

and
C_{12}
is a constant defined later.

Under the event of
{\mathcal{E}^{m}}
defined in (
D.21
),
by Lemma
G.3
, we know that
f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}^{\star})
is
\mu
-strongly convex and
L
-smooth with respect to
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}
with probability at least
1-M^{-1}\exp\{-k_{m}(s^{\star}+1)\}
, where

\mu=\frac{p(s^{\star}+1)\nu_{x}\nu_{b}}{4},\quad L=\frac{3p(s^{\star}+1)\rho_{x}\rho_{p}}{4}.

Define

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{3\nu_{x}\nu_{b}\rho_{x}\rho_{b}}{16},\quad\Upsilon_{2}=\frac{\nu_{x}\nu_{b}+3\rho_{x}\rho_{b}}{4}.

Therefore, by Lemma
I.3
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{12,m}

\displaystyle=\langle{\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}^{\star})-\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})},{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{p(s^{\star}+1)\Upsilon_{1}}{\Upsilon_{2}}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{p(s^{\star}+1)\Upsilon_{2}}\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}^{\star})-\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{F}^{2},

with probability at least
1-M^{-1}\exp\{-k_{m}(s^{\star}+1)\}
, the probability that
{\mathcal{E}^{m}}
happens.

Define
\|\cdot\|_{*}
to be the nuclear norm. In the last step, we apply Hölder’s inequality and obtain,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{13,m}

\displaystyle=\langle{\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})},{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{*}

\displaystyle\geq-\sqrt{2k}\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\geq-\sqrt{2k}\left\{\frac{1}{2C_{13}}\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{C_{13}}{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}\right\},

and the second last step follows by the fact
\|\mathbf{Z}\|_{*}\leq\sqrt{r}\|\mathbf{Z}\|_{F}
, where
r
is the rank of
\mathbf{Z}
. Since
k\leq k_{m}
for all
m=1,\ldots,M
and hence the rank of
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}
is at most
2k
.
C_{13}>0
will be defined below.

Setting
C_{12}
and
C_{13}
as

C_{12}=\frac{2\Upsilon_{4}\Upsilon_{3}\sqrt{s^{\star}}}{\Upsilon_{6}},\quad C_{13}=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2k}}\frac{p(s^{\star}+1)\Upsilon_{1}}{\Upsilon_{2}},

where
\Upsilon_{6}=\frac{\nu_{x}\nu_{a}}{2}
.

Combining results of
\mathcal{I}_{11,m}
,
\mathcal{I}_{12,m}
, and
\mathcal{I}_{13,m}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1,m}

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{3p(s^{\star}+1)\Upsilon_{1}}{4\Upsilon_{2}}-\frac{ps^{\star}\Upsilon_{4}^{2}\Upsilon_{3}^{2}}{\Upsilon_{6}}\right)\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{\Upsilon_{6}}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2k\Upsilon_{2}}{p(s^{\star}+1)\Upsilon_{1}}\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{p(s^{\star}+1)\Upsilon_{2}}\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}^{\star})-\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{F}^{2},

with probability at least
1-M^{-1}\exp\{-k_{m}(s^{\star}+1)\}
.
∎

Lemma D.7
.

Let
\Upsilon_{5}
be a constant defined in Section
C
. Under conditions of Lemma
D.1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2,m}

\displaystyle\leq 3\bigg\{\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}^{\star})-\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+p(s^{\star}+1)\Upsilon_{5}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}+k\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{2}^{2}\bigg\}.

Proof of Lemma
D.7
.

By definition of
\mathcal{I}_{2,m}
, we can write

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2,m}

\displaystyle=\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\bigg\{\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\}_{m=1}^{M}\cup\mathcal{A}^{\backslash m\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\}_{m=1}^{M}\cup\mathcal{A}^{\backslash m\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})-\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{F}^{2}\bigg\}.

By (
D.3
) and triangle inequality, we can first decompose
\mathcal{I}_{21,m}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{21,m}

\displaystyle=\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\}_{m=1}^{M}\cup\mathcal{A}^{\backslash m\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}(\mathbf{e}_{j}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{k})\{\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star})+(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star\top})\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}(\mathbf{e}_{j}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{k_{m}})\right\|_{F}.

Since
(\mathbf{e}_{j}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{k})\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}={\bf 0}
and
(\mathbf{e}_{j}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{k})(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star\top})={\bf 0}
for
j\not\in\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}\backslash\{i\}
, we can write the above display as

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j\in\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}\cup\{i\}}(\mathbf{e}_{j}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{k})\{\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star})+(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star\top})\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}(\mathbf{e}_{j}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{k_{m}})\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})(s^{\star}+1)}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\{\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star})+(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star\top})\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{F},

where the last inequality follows by
|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}\cup\{i\}|\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})(s^{\star}+1)
.
Now, consider single
i\in\{1,\ldots,p\}
and apply triangle inequality once more, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{211,m,i}

\displaystyle=\|\{\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star})+(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\top}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star\top})\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq(\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|_{2}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2})\|(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\|_{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}.

Using the fact that for a pair of matrices
\mathbf{X}
,
\mathbf{Y}
,
\|\mathbf{X}\otimes\mathbf{Y}\|_{2}=\|\mathbf{X}\|_{2}\|\mathbf{Y}\|_{2}
, the above display is equivalent as

\displaystyle=(\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|_{2}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2})\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}\|_{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq(1+C_{A})\|\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}(\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|_{2}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2})\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F},

where the last step follows by the assumption stated in Lemma
D.1
:
\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}\leq C_{A}\|\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}
.

Note that, by triangle inequality and the definition of spectral norm , we can write

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|_{2}\leq\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{B}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}\leq(C_{B}+1)\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}.

(D.25)

Plugging (
D.25
) back to
\mathcal{I}_{211,m,i}
yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{211,m,i}

\displaystyle\leq(1+C_{A})(2+C_{B})\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}\|\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F},

(D.26)

where it follows by the fact that
\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}
.

Then, sum up
\mathcal{I}_{211,m,i}
for
i\in V
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{21,m}

\displaystyle\leq\Upsilon_{5}^{1/2}\sqrt{s^{\star}+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F},

where
\Upsilon_{5}=(1+C_{A})^{2}(2+C_{B})^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}^{2}\rho_{b}\rho_{a}
.

Then, applying Cauchy-Schwarz inequality yields

\mathcal{I}_{21,m}^{2}\leq p(s^{\star}+1)\Upsilon_{5}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}.

Therefore, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2,m}

\displaystyle\leq 3\bigg\{\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}^{\star})-\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\Upsilon_{5}p(s^{\star}+1)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}+k\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{2}^{2}\bigg\}.

∎

Lemma D.8
.

The constants
\Upsilon_{3},\Upsilon_{4},\Upsilon_{6}
are defined in Section
C
.
Under the conditions of Lemma
D.2
, we have

\mathcal{I}_{3,i}\geq\frac{M\Upsilon_{6}}{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{s^{\star}\Upsilon_{4}^{2}\Upsilon_{3}^{2}}{\Upsilon_{6}}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}\\
-\frac{(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}}{M\Upsilon_{6}}\|\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{r(k,\infty)}^{2}.

with probability at least
1-p^{-1}\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\exp(-k_{m}s^{\star})
.

Proof of Lemma
D.8
.

By definition of
\mathcal{I}_{3,i}
, we can write

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{3,i}

\displaystyle=\langle{[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle{[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle{[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle{[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}_{31,i}+\mathcal{I}_{32,i}+\mathcal{I}_{33,i}.

First, we express
\mathcal{I}_{31,i}
as (
D.4
) and obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{31,i}

\displaystyle=\langle{[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{m=1}^{M}\langle{[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{m=1}^{M}\langle{[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{m=1}^{M}\langle{[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{m=1}^{M}\langle{[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle.

Apply Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we can lower bound the above display as

\displaystyle\geq-\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}.

(D.27)

We bound the above two terms individually. Following the same proof steps as in (
D.6
), for
m=1,\ldots,M
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}

\displaystyle\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}\tau_{2}}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\|_{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|_{F}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}\tau_{2}}(C_{B}+1)\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\|_{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}.

(D.28)

We can bound the second term in (
D.27
) similar in the way shown in (
D.24
) and obtain

\displaystyle\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}

\displaystyle(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}\tau_{2}}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\|_{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|_{1}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})s^{\star}\alpha^{\star}\tau_{2}}(1+C_{B})\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}\|_{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{1}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}.

(D.29)

Combining results from (
D.28
)–(
D.6
), we can apply Young’s inequality and lower bound
\mathcal{I}_{31,i}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{31,i}

\displaystyle\geq-\sum_{m=1}^{M}\Upsilon_{4}\Upsilon_{3}\sqrt{s^{\star}}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\geq-\Upsilon_{4}\Upsilon_{3}\sqrt{s^{\star}}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\left(\frac{1}{2C_{31}}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{C_{31}}{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}\right),

where
\Upsilon_{3}
, and
\Upsilon_{4}
are defined in Section
C
and
C_{31}
is defined below.

We define the event
\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{i}
:

\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{i}:=\cap_{m=1}^{M}{\mathcal{E}_{i}^{m}}(\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}),

(D.30)

where
{\mathcal{E}_{i}^{m}}(\cdot)
for
m=1,\ldots,M
are defined in (
D.20
). Then, taking the union bound, if
N=O(\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\kappa_{m}^{2}(k_{m}s^{\star}+\log M+\log p))
, we see that event
\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{i}
will happen with probability at least 1-(pM)^-1∑_m=1^Mexp{-k_m s^⋆}≥1-p^-1max_m=1,…,Mexp(-k_m s^⋆).

To find the lower bound of
\mathcal{I}_{32,i}
, we use formula of
\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f
stated in (
D.4
). Combining with the fact that

-(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star})(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}})\mathbf{Y}^{m}=(\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}}){\mathbf{Y}_{\backslash\{i\}}^{m}}

for
m=1,\ldots,M
, we can write

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{32,i}

\displaystyle=\langle{[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=1}^{M}\langle{-(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star})(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})},{[\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=1}^{M}\langle{[\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})},{[\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|[\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}}){\mathbf{Y}_{\backslash\{i\}}^{m}}\|_{F}^{2}.

Therefore, we can further lower bound
\mathcal{I}_{32,i}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{32,i}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{A}^{m\star})\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}})\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}.

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\nu_{x}}{2}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{A}^{m\star})\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}=M\Upsilon_{6}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2},

with probability at least
1-p^{-1}\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\exp(-k_{m}s^{\star})
. The last inequality is followed by conditioning on the event
\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{i}
in (
D.30
).

Finally, applying Lemma
I.6
yields,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{33,i}

\displaystyle=\langle{[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}},{\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{r(k,\infty)}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{r(k,1)}

\displaystyle\geq-\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{r(k,\infty)}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{r(k,2)}

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{r(k,\infty)}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\geq-\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}}\left\{\frac{1}{2C_{33}}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{r(k,\infty)}^{2}+\frac{C_{33}}{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}\right\}.

Setting
C_{31}
,
C_{33}
as

C_{31}=\frac{\Upsilon_{6}}{2\Upsilon_{3}\Upsilon_{4}\sqrt{s^{\star}}},\quad C_{33}=\frac{M\Upsilon_{6}}{2\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}}},

Combining
\mathcal{I}_{31,i}
,
\mathcal{I}_{32,i}
,
\mathcal{I}_{33,i}
,
\mathcal{I}_{33,i}
obtains

\mathcal{I}_{3,i}\geq\frac{M\Upsilon_{6}}{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{s^{\star}\Upsilon_{4}^{2}\Upsilon_{3}^{2}}{\Upsilon_{6}}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}\\
-\frac{(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}}{M\Upsilon_{6}}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{r(k,\infty)}^{2}.

with probability at least
1-p^{-1}\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\exp(-k_{m}s^{\star})
.

∎

Lemma D.9
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
D.2
, we have

\mathcal{I}_{4,i}\leq 3\bigg\{\Upsilon_{5}Ms^{\star}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}+\Upsilon_{7}M^{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}\\
+(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{r(k,\infty)}^{2}\bigg\}.

Proof of Lemma
D.9
.

We want to find the upper bound of
\mathcal{I}_{4,i}
. By definition of
\mathcal{I}_{4,i}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{4,i}

\displaystyle=\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\bigg\{\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 3(\mathcal{I}_{41,i}+\mathcal{I}_{42,i}+\mathcal{I}_{43,i}).

We further upper bound
\mathcal{I}_{41,i}
and
\mathcal{I}_{42,i}
, respectively below.

First, we can write
\mathcal{I}_{41,i}
using the result in (
D.27
):

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{41,i}

\displaystyle=\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{m=1}^{M}[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}^{\top}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\bigg\|_{F}^{2}.

Apply Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the above display can be bounded as

\displaystyle\leq 2M\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2M\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}.

(D.31)

Then, we bound the above two terms separately.

Since
\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}
is evaluated on the support
\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}
, for any
(u,v)\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}
, we have
\lceil v/k\rceil\in\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{|\bar{\mathscr{N}}_{i}|}\otimes{(\mathbf{A}^{m\top}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top})}\}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}.

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}\|_{2}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}(1+C_{A})^{2}(2+C_{B})^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2},

(D.32)

where the last line follows by the condition of Lemma
D.2
.

Similarly, we can write the second term of (
D.31
) as

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i\mathscr{N}_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{|\mathscr{N}_{i}|}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\mathscr{N}_{i}\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}.

Using the fact that
|\mathscr{N}_{i}|\leq\vartheta_{1}s^{\star}/2\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}
, we have
\|\{\mathbf{I}_{|\mathscr{N}_{i}|}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}
. Therefore, the above display can be further bounded as

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}(2+C_{B})^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}.

(D.33)

Plugging the results of (
D.32
)–(
D.33
) back to (
D.31
), we could conclude that

\mathcal{I}_{41,i}\leq M\Upsilon_{5}\sum_{m=1}^{M}s^{\star}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2},

where
\Upsilon_{5}=4(1+C_{A})^{2}(2+C_{B})^{2}\rho_{b}\rho_{a}\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}^{2}
.

Similarly, expanding
\mathcal{I}_{42,ij}
obtains

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{42,i}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{m=1}^{M}(\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\backslash\{i\}\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq M\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}^{4}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\backslash\{i\}\backslash\{i\}}\|_{2}^{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\Upsilon_{7}M^{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2},

where
\Upsilon_{7}=\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\rho_{a}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}^{2}
.

Next, we can write
\mathcal{I}_{43,i}
as

\mathcal{I}_{43,i}=\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{r(k,\infty)}^{2}.

Combining results of
\mathcal{I}_{41,i}
and
\mathcal{I}_{42,i}
, we can conclude that

\mathcal{I}_{4,i}\leq 3\bigg\{\Upsilon_{5}Ms^{\star}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}+M^{2}\Upsilon_{7}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}\\
+(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}f(\mathbf{A}^{\star},\mathbf{B}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{r(k,\infty)}^{2}\bigg\}.

∎

Appendix E
Analysis of the Initialization

Section
E.1
discusses the proof of Theorem
5.1
. Section
E.2
introduces auxiliary lemmas for the proofs of Theorem
5.1
.
Section
E.3
shows the proof of Lemma
5.2
.

E.1
Proof of Theorem
5.1

We prove the result for one modality.
Let
\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}=(\mathbf{L}^{\star})^{\dagger}
be the pseudoinverse of
\mathbf{L}^{m\star}
, where
\mathbf{L}^{m\star}\in\mathbb{R}^{k_{m}\times k}
be the matrix realization of
{\mathscr{L}^{m}}
such that the
\ell\ell^{\prime}
-th entry of
\mathbf{L}^{m\star}
is
L_{\ell\ell^{\prime}}^{m}=\langle{{\mathscr{L}^{m}}\phi_{\ell^{\prime}}^{m}},{\phi_{\ell}^{m}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}_{m}}
. It should be noted that
\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times k_{m}}
is different from
\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times k_{m}}
: the former is first truncating
{\mathscr{L}^{m}}
and then taking pseudoinverse while the later is first taking the pseudoinverse of
{\mathscr{L}^{m}}
and then conducting finite truncation. However, the difference of the two will become small as
k
and
k_{m}
are selected large enough and the remaining terms are small in magnitude.
For the estimate in (
4.3
), we have the following

\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}=\|\mathbf{Q}{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}\leq\|\mathbf{Q}{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}\|_{F}+\|{\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}}\|_{F},\qquad m=1,2,

(E.1)

where
\mathbf{Q}
is a diagonal matrix with diagonal entries taking values in
\{-1,1\}
that aligns columns of the matrix
\mathbf{V}^{m}
with the corresponding columns of the population canonical matrix
\mathbf{V}^{m\star}
so that
\langle{q_{j}\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m}},{\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m\star}}\rangle\geq 0
. For the first term of (
E.1
), we can apply the result of Lemma
E.1
to find a valid upper bound. For the second term, we recall that

\displaystyle{\mathscr{L}^{k_{m},k}}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{k_{m}}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{k}\langle{{\mathscr{L}^{m}}\phi_{\ell^{\prime}}},{\phi_{\ell}^{m}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}_{m}}\phi_{\ell}^{m}\otimes\phi_{\ell^{\prime}};

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{m}^{k,k_{m}}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{k_{m}}\langle{\mathscr{A}_{m}\phi_{\ell^{\prime}}^{m}},{\phi_{\ell}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\phi_{\ell}\otimes\phi_{\ell^{\prime}}^{m}.

Define
{\mathscr{L}^{m,r}}={\mathscr{L}^{m}}-{\mathscr{L}^{k_{m},k}}
and write

\displaystyle\|\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq(2k)^{1/2}\|\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=(2k)^{1/2}|\!|\!|({\mathscr{L}^{k_{m},k}})^{\dagger}-\mathscr{A}_{m}^{k,k_{m}}|\!|\!|.

Apply Lemma
E.5
, the above display can be bounded as

\displaystyle\leq 2^{3/2}k^{1/2}|\!|\!|{\mathscr{L}^{m}}^{\dagger}|\!|\!|^{2}|\!|\!|{\mathscr{L}^{m,r}}|\!|\!|.

Hence, we complete the proof.

E.2
Proofs of Lemma
E.1
–
E.6

Lemma E.1
.

Suppose that Assumptions
5
–
6
hold and
N=O(\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\kappa_{m}^{2}k_{m})
where
\kappa_{m}=\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m})/\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m})
. Then, let
C_{\gamma_{k},\nu_{x},\rho_{x}}>0
be a constant depending on
\gamma_{k}
,
\nu_{x}
, and
\rho_{x}
, we have

\|\mathbf{Q}{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}\|_{F}\leq C_{\gamma_{k},\nu,\rho}\left(1+\max_{j=1,\ldots,k}\max_{j\neq i}\frac{1}{|\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{i}|}\right)\sqrt{k\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{2}k_{m}}{N}},

with probability at least
1-5\exp(-\sum_{m=1}^{2}k_{m})
.

Proof of Lemma
E.1
.

We have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{Q}{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m\top}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}-\mathbf{Q}\bm{\Gamma}^{\star-1/2}\mathbf{V}^{m\star\top}(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle(\mathbf{Q},\bm{\Gamma}\text{ are diagonal matrices})

\displaystyle=\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m\top}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}-\bm{\Gamma}^{\star-1/2}\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{V}^{m\star\top}(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m\top}\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m}-\mathbf{V}^{m\star}\mathbf{Q}\right)^{\top}(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\left(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}-\bm{\Gamma}^{\star-1/2}\right)\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{V}^{m\star\top}(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\|_{F}.

We then apply Lemma
E.2
–
E.4
to upper bound the above three terms individually and arrive at

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{Q}{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|_{2}(\rho_{x}^{1/2}\nu_{x}^{-2}+\nu_{x}^{-3/2})\sqrt{\frac{kk_{m}}{N}}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{2}k^{1/2}\nu_{x}^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|_{2}(\gamma_{k}^{-3/2}+1)\max_{j=1,\ldots,k}\max_{j\neq i}\frac{1}{|\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{i}|}\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{12}-\mathbf{R}^{12\star}\|_{2},

with probability at least
1-\exp(-k_{m})
.

Under the condition that
N=O(\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\kappa_{m}^{2}k_{m})
, we could apply Lemma
E.6
and obtain

\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{12}-\mathbf{R}^{12\star}\|_{2}^{2}\leq C_{3}\rho_{x}^{4}\nu_{x}^{-4}(\rho_{x}^{1/2}\nu_{x}^{-1/2}+1)^{2}\frac{\sum_{m=1}^{2}k_{m}}{N}

with probability at least
1-3\exp(-\sum_{m=1}^{2}k_{m})
for some constant
C_{3}>0
.

Recall that

\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|_{2}=\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{12}^{-1/2}\|_{2}
and by Lemma
I.1
, we have
\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|_{2}\leq(3/2)\gamma_{k}^{-1/2}
, with probability at least
1-\exp(-k)
.

Taking the union bound, we can conclude that

\|\mathbf{Q}{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\check{\mathbf{A}}^{m\star}\|_{F}\leq C_{\gamma_{k},\rho_{x},\nu_{x}}\left(1+\max_{j=1,\ldots,k}\max_{j\neq i}\frac{1}{|\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{i}|}\right)\sqrt{\frac{k\sum_{k=1}^{2}k_{m}}{N}},

with probability at least
1-5\exp(-\sum_{m=1}^{2}k_{m})
and

\displaystyle C_{\gamma_{k},\rho_{x},\nu_{x}}

\displaystyle=C_{4}\bigg\{\gamma_{k}^{-1/2}(\rho_{x}^{1/2}\nu_{x}^{-2}+\nu_{x}^{-3/2})+\rho_{x}^{2}\nu_{x}^{-5/2}(\gamma_{k}^{-1/2}+\gamma_{k}^{-2})(\rho_{x}^{1/2}\nu_{x}^{-1/2}+1)\bigg\},

for some universal constant
C_{4}>0
.
∎

Lemma E.2
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
E.1
and an universal constant
C_{1}>0
, we have

\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m\top}\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\}\right\|_{F}\leq C_{1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|_{2}(\rho_{x}^{1/2}\nu_{x}^{-2}+\nu_{x}^{-3/2})\sqrt{\frac{kk_{m}}{N}},

with probability at least
1-\exp(-k_{m})
.

Proof of Lemma
E.2
.

We have

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m\top}\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m\top}\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq k^{1/2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\|_{2},

where the inequality follows by the fact that
\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m}\|_{2}\leq 1
. Apply Lemma
I.10
, we can further obtain that

\displaystyle\leq k^{1/2}\left\{3\left\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m}\right\|_{2}\vee\left\|\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m}\right\|_{2}\right)+1\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-3/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m}-\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m}\right\|_{2}.

Recall that
\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m}_{11})\geq\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m})\geq\nu_{x}
and
\rho_{x}\geq\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m})\geq\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m}_{11})
.
Since
N=O(\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\kappa_{m}^{2}k_{m})
, we have

3\rho_{x}/2\geq\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{11})\geq\nu_{x}/2,

(E.2)

with probability at least
1-\exp(-k_{m})
, following Lemma
I.1
.

Therefore, under Assumption
6
, we have

\displaystyle\left\{3\left\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m}\right\|_{2}\vee\left\|\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m}\right\|_{2}\right)+1\right\}\left\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-3/2}\right\|_{2}\leq 9\rho_{x}^{1/2}\nu_{x}^{-2}+\nu_{x}^{-3/2},

(E.3)

with probability at least
1-\exp(-k_{m})
.
Then, we arrive at the conclusion that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m\top}\left((\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right)\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|_{2}(\rho_{x}^{1/2}\nu_{x}^{-2}+\nu_{x}^{-3/2})\sqrt{\frac{kk_{m}}{N}},

with probability at least
1-\exp(-k_{m})
.
∎

Lemma E.3
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
E.1
and an universal constant
C_{1}>0
, we have

\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m}-\mathbf{V}^{m\star}\mathbf{Q}\right)^{\top}(\bm{\Sigma}_{i,i}^{m})^{-1/2}\|_{F}\\
\leq C_{1}k^{1/2}\nu_{x}^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|_{2}\max_{j=1,\ldots,k}\max_{j\neq i}\frac{1}{|\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{i}|}\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{12}-\mathbf{R}^{12\star}\|_{2}.

Proof of Lemma
E.3
.

Write

\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m}-\mathbf{V}^{m\star}\mathbf{Q}\right)^{\top}(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\|_{F}\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|_{2}\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\|_{2}\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m}-\mathbf{V}^{m\star}\mathbf{Q}\|_{F}.

Note that we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{m}-\mathbf{V}^{m\star}\mathbf{Q}\|_{F}^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{i}^{m}-q_{i}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m\star}\|_{2}^{2},

where
q_{i}
is the
i
th diagonal entry of
\mathbf{Q}
.

Since
q_{i}
is either
+1
or
-1
such that
\|\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{i}^{m}-q_{i}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m\star}\|_{2}^{2}
is minimized, we know that
\langle{\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{i}^{m}},{q_{i}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m\star}}\rangle\geq 0
. Let
\Delta=\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{12}-\mathbf{R}^{12\star}
and
C_{2},C_{3}>0
be universal constants.
Recall the singular decomposition of
\mathbf{R}^{12\star}
is
\mathbf{R}^{12\star}=\sum_{j}^{k}\gamma_{j}\mathbf{v}_{j}^{1\star}(\mathbf{v}_{j}^{2\star})^{\top}
, where
\gamma_{j}
could be viewed as the canonical correlation of
\mathbf{y}_{1}^{1}
and
{\mathbf{y}_{1}^{2}}
for
j=1,\ldots,k
. Without the loss of generality, let
m=1
be the first data modality and
m^{\prime}=2
be the second data modality, we can apply Theorem 5.2.2 in
Hsing and Eubank 2015
, restated in Lemma
I.9
, and obtain

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{i}^{m}-q_{i}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m\star}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\sum_{j\neq i}\frac{\gamma_{j}\langle{\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m^{\prime}}q_{j}},{\Delta^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m\star}q_{i}}\rangle+\gamma_{i}\langle{\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m^{\prime}}q_{i}},{\Delta^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m\star}q_{j}}\rangle}{\gamma_{j}^{2}-\gamma_{i}^{2}}(\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m\star}q_{j})\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{2}\max_{j\neq i}\frac{2}{|\gamma_{j}^{2}-\gamma_{i}^{2}|}\|\Delta\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\neq i}\frac{\gamma_{j}}{|\gamma_{j}^{2}-\gamma_{i}^{2}|}\bigg\|\sum_{j\neq i}\langle{\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m^{\prime}}q_{j}},{\Delta^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m\star}q_{i}}\rangle(\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m\star}q_{j})\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\max_{j\neq i}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{|\gamma_{j}^{2}-\gamma_{i}^{2}|}\bigg\|\sum_{j\neq i}{\langle{\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m^{\prime}}q_{i}},{\Delta^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m\star}q_{j}}\rangle}(\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m\star}q_{j})\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{2}\max_{j\neq i}\frac{2}{|\gamma_{j}^{2}-\gamma_{i}^{2}|}\|\Delta\|_{2}.

Since
\|\sum_{j\neq i}{\langle{\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m^{\prime}}q_{i}},{\Delta^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m\star}q_{j}}\rangle}(\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m\star}q_{j})\|_{2}\leq\|\Delta\|_{2}
,
\|\sum_{j\neq i}\langle{\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m^{\prime}}q_{j}},{\Delta^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m\star}q_{i}}\rangle(\mathbf{v}_{j}^{m\star}q_{j})\|_{2}\leq\|\Delta\|_{2}
and
\gamma_{i},\gamma_{j}\leq 1
, the above display can be further bounded as

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\neq i}\frac{2\|\Delta\|_{2}}{|\gamma_{j}^{2}-\gamma_{i}^{2}|}+C_{2}\max_{j\neq i}\frac{2}{|\gamma_{j}^{2}-\gamma_{i}^{2}|}\|\Delta\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{3}\max_{j\neq i}\frac{1}{|\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{i}|}\|\Delta\|_{2},

where the last inequality follows by the fact that the magnitude of the canonical correlation is less than one
|\gamma_{i}|\leq 1
for any
i
and hence
|\gamma_{j}^{2}-\gamma_{i}^{2}|\leq|\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{i}||\gamma_{j}+\gamma_{i}|\leq 2|\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{i}|
. Then, we complete the proof.

∎

Lemma E.4
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
E.1
and an universal constant
C_{1}>0
, we have

\|\left(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}-\bm{\Gamma}^{\star-1/2}\right)\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{V}^{m\star\top}(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\|_{F}\leq k^{1/2}\gamma_{k}^{-3/2}\nu_{x}^{-1/2}(3\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|+1)\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{12}-\mathbf{R}^{12\star}\|_{2}.

Proof of Lemma
E.4
.

Since
\mathbf{Q}
is a diagonal matrix whose diagonal entries taking values in
\{1,-1\}
and
\|\mathbf{V}^{m\star\top}\|_{2}\leq 1
, we can write

\left\|\left(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}-\bm{\Gamma}^{\star-1/2}\right)\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{V}^{m\star\top}(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\right\|_{F}\leq\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{11}^{m})^{-1/2}\|_{2}\left\|{\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}-\bm{\Gamma}^{\star-1/2}}\right\|_{F}.

Applying Lemma
I.10
again with
\lambda=(\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Gamma}^{\star}\|_{2})\leq 1
yields

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}-\bm{\Gamma}^{\star-1/2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq k^{1/2}\|\bm{\Gamma}^{\star-3/2}\|(3\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|+1)\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}-\bm{\Gamma}^{\star}\|_{2}.

\displaystyle\leq k^{1/2}\gamma_{k}^{-3/2}(3\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{-1/2}\|+1)\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}-\bm{\Gamma}^{\star}\|_{2}.

(E.4)

Applying Weyl’s inequality (See Theorem III.2.1 in
Bhatia 2013
for details), we have

\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}-\bm{\Gamma}^{\star}\|_{2}\leq\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{12}-\mathbf{R}^{12\star}\|_{2}.

Then, we complete the proof.

∎

Lemma E.5
.

Let
{\mathscr{L}}\in\mathfrak{B}(\mathbb{H}_{1},\mathbb{H}_{2})
be a compact operator and
\{\phi_{1,\ell}\}_{\ell\in\mathbb{N}}
and
\{\phi_{2,\ell}\}_{\ell\in\mathbb{N}}
be the CONS for
\mathbb{H}_{1}
and
\mathbb{H}_{2}
, respectively.
Let

{\mathscr{L}^{r}}={\mathscr{L}}-{\mathscr{L}^{p,d}},\quad{\mathscr{L}^{p,d}}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{p}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{d}\langle{{\mathscr{L}}\phi_{1,\ell^{\prime}}},{\phi_{2,\ell}}\rangle\phi_{2,\ell}\otimes\phi_{1,\ell^{\prime}}.

Define
\mathscr{A}=\mathscr{L}^{\dagger}
and
\mathscr{A}^{d,p}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{d}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{p}\langle{{\mathscr{L}^{\dagger}}\phi_{2,\ell^{\prime}}},{\phi_{1,\ell}}\rangle\phi_{1,\ell}\otimes\phi_{2,\ell^{\prime}}
, then we have

\|(\mathscr{L}^{p,d})^{\dagger}-\mathscr{A}^{d,p}\|_{\text{HS}}\leq 2|\!|\!|{\mathscr{L}}^{\dagger}|\!|\!|^{2}|\!|\!|{\mathscr{L}^{r}}|\!|\!|.

Proof of Lemma
E.5
.

Write

\displaystyle|\!|\!|(\mathscr{L}^{p,d})^{\dagger}-\mathscr{A}^{d,p}|\!|\!|

\displaystyle\leq|\!|\!|({\mathscr{L}^{p,d}})^{\dagger}-\mathscr{A}|\!|\!|

\displaystyle=|\!|\!|({\mathscr{L}^{p,d}})^{\dagger}-({\mathscr{L}^{p,d}}+{\mathscr{L}^{r}})^{\dagger}|\!|\!|.

Apply the result from Lemma
I.8
, we could further bound the above display as

\displaystyle\leq 2(|\!|\!|({\mathscr{L}^{p,d}})^{\dagger}|\!|\!|^{2}\vee|\!|\!|{\mathscr{L}}^{\dagger}|\!|\!|^{2})|\!|\!|{\mathscr{L}^{r}}|\!|\!|

\displaystyle\leq 2|\!|\!|{\mathscr{L}}^{\dagger}|\!|\!|^{2}|\!|\!|{\mathscr{L}^{r}}|\!|\!|.

Hence we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma E.6
.

Given two centered Gaussian random vectors
\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
with covariance
\bm{\Sigma}_{x}
and
\bm{\Sigma}_{y}
, respectively. We define
\bm{\Sigma}_{xy}
be the cross covariance and
\mathbf{R}_{xy}=\bm{\Sigma}_{x}^{-1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{xy}\bm{\Sigma}_{y}^{-1/2}
. Let
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{x}
be the sample covariance of
\mathbf{x}
,
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{y}
sample covariance of
\mathbf{y}
, and
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{xy}
be the sample cross covariance of
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}
with
N
independent samples. Define
\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{xy}=\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{x}^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{xy}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{y}^{-1/2}
. Let
C_{1}>0
be an universal constant. Assume that
N=O(p+d)
,
\alpha=(\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Sigma}_{y}\|_{2})
and
\beta=(\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}^{-1/2}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Sigma}_{y}^{-1/2}\|_{2})
, then

\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{xy}-\mathbf{R}_{xy}\|_{2}\leq C_{1}\alpha^{2}\beta^{4}(3\alpha^{1/2}\beta+1)\sqrt{\frac{(p+d)}{N}},

with probability at least
1-3\exp\{-(p+d)\}
.

Proof.

The inequality could be shown by first applying the triangle inequality and then use the tail bound to find the upper bound of each term individually. We have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{xy}-\mathbf{R}_{xy}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{x}^{-1/2}-\bm{\Sigma}_{x}^{-1/2})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{xy}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{y}^{-1/2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}^{-1/2}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{xy}-\bm{\Sigma}_{xy})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{y}^{-1/2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}^{-1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{xy}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{y}^{-1/2}-\bm{\Sigma}_{y}^{-1/2})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{I}_{9,1}+\mathcal{I}_{9,2}+\mathcal{I}_{9,3}.

Since
N=O(d+p)
, we have

\displaystyle(i)\quad\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{x})\geq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}_{x});

\displaystyle(ii)\quad\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{y})\geq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}_{y});

\displaystyle(iii)\quad\sigma_{\max}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{xy})\leq\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}_{xy})\leq\frac{3}{2}(\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Sigma}_{y}\|_{2}),

with probability at least
1-3\exp\{-(d+p)\}
, following Lemma
I.1
and the union bound.

Apply Lemma
I.10
and Lemma
I.1
to
\mathcal{I}_{9,1}
and we could obtain the upper bound

\mathcal{I}_{9,1}\leq C_{2}(\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Sigma}_{y}\|_{2})^{2}(\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}^{-1/2}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Sigma}_{y}^{-1/2}\|_{2})^{4}\\
\left\{3(\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Sigma}_{y}\|_{2})^{1/2}(\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}^{-1/2}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Sigma}_{y}^{-1/2}\|_{2})+1\right\}\sqrt{\frac{(p+d)}{N}},

with probability
1-3\exp\{-(d+p)\}
for some absolute constant
C_{2}>0
. We obtain the same upper bound for
\mathcal{I}_{9,3}
. For
\mathcal{I}_{9,2}
, we have smaller coefficient:

\mathcal{I}_{9,2}\leq C_{3}(\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Sigma}_{y}\|_{2})(\|\bm{\Sigma}_{x}^{-1/2}\|_{2}\vee\|\bm{\Sigma}_{y}^{-1/2}\|_{2})^{2}\sqrt{\frac{(p+d)}{N}},

with probability
1-2\exp\{-(d+p)\}
and
C_{3}>0
.
Hence, the terms
\mathcal{I}_{9,1}
and
\mathcal{I}_{9,3}
dominate. Then, by summing up
\mathcal{I}_{9,1}
,
\mathcal{I}_{9,2}
, and
\mathcal{I}_{9,3}
, we complete the proof.

∎

E.3
Proof of Lemma
5.2

The proof is consisted of three steps. In the first step, we show the condition where the solution is nontrivial. In the second and third step, we show the contraction of the iterates of Algorithm
2
.

Step 1
. Given
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}
for
m=1,\ldots,M
, we want to verify that
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}{\mathbf{Y}_{i}^{m}}\neq{\bf 0}
for
m=1,\ldots,M
and
i\in V
with high probability. Then the solution to the minimizer of
h_{i}(\mathbf{B}_{i})
is nontrivial. Since

\frac{1}{N}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}{\mathbf{Y}_{i}^{m}}\|_{F}^{2}=\VEC({\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)})^{\top}(\mathbf{I}_{k}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{ii})\VEC({\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)})\geq 0

and
\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{I}_{k}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{ii})=\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{ii})
, it suffices to show that
\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{ii})>0
. We define the event as

{\mathcal{E}_{ii}^{m}}=\{\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{ii})>0\},\quad m=1,\ldots,M.

Then, under the condition that
N=O(\kappa_{m}^{2}(k_{m}+\log M+\log p))
, Lemma
I.1
states that event
{\mathcal{E}_{ii}^{m}}
happens with probability at least
1-(pM)^{-1}\exp(-k_{m})
for
m=1,\ldots,M
.
Taking the union bound again and applying De Morgan’s law, we can show that the event

\cap_{m=1}^{M}\cap_{i=1}^{p}{\mathcal{E}_{ii}^{m}},

happens with probability at least
1-\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\exp(-k_{m})
.
Then, we conclude that
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}{\mathbf{Y}_{i}^{m}}\neq{\bf 0}
for
i\in V
and
m=1,\ldots,M
with probability at least

1-max_m=1,…,Mexp(-k_m)≥1-max_m=1,…,Mexp(-k_m s^⋆).

Step 2
. Given that the solution is nontrivial, the next step is to prove that the projected gradient descent would converge to a local optimum with quantified error.
By Lemma
G.5
, we know that
h
is a

\min_{m=1,\ldots,M}2^{-1}\nu_{x}\sigma_{\min}^{2}({\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)})
-strongly convex with respect to
\mathbf{B}_{i}
with probability at least
1-\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\exp(-k_{m}s^{\star})
. It is easy to verify that
h
is
\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\sigma_{\max}^{2}(\mathbf{A}^{m(0)})\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|
-smooth. Let
\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}^{m}=\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}{\mathbf{Y}_{i}^{m}}
and
\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{m}=\mathbf{A}^{m\star}{\mathbf{Y}_{i}^{m}}
for
i\in V
. Then, we can write

h(\mathbf{B}_{i})=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}^{m}-\sum_{j\in i\in V}\mathbf{B}_{ij}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{j}^{m}\|_{F}^{2}.

Let
\pi_{0}=C_{\vartheta}\left\{1-{\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{A}^{m^{\prime}(0)})\nu_{x}\eta_{B_{0}}}/2\right\}
.
Apply Lemma
E.7
–
E.8
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{+}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\pi_{0}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta_{B_{0}}C_{\vartheta}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\big\{\|[-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}})^{\top}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}^{\top}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\big\}

\displaystyle\quad+{C_{\delta,i}C_{\vartheta}\eta_{B_{0}}}\sqrt{\frac{\alpha^{\star}s^{\star}k^{2}}{N}},

(E.5)

with probability at least
1-3\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\exp(-k_{m}s^{\star})
.

Note that we can write

\displaystyle\|[-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}})^{\top}+\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}})^{\top}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\big\{\|[-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\{\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}})^{\top}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}(\mathbf{I}_{p}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star}})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\cdot\backslash\{i\}}\{\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{({\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star})}\}^{\top}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\big\}

\displaystyle\leq\rho_{b}^{1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\left\{\sigma_{\max}({\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)})+\rho_{a}^{1/2}\right\}\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\gamma}\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F},

(E.6)

where
C_{\gamma}=\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\rho_{b}^{1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}\|_{2}\left\{\sigma_{\max}({\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)})+\rho_{a}^{1/2}\right\}\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{1})s^{\star}}
.

Combine (
E.5
)–(
E.6
) together, we have

\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{+}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}\leq\pi_{0}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}+\frac{C_{\gamma}C_{\vartheta}\eta_{B_{0}}}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}+{C_{\delta,i}C_{\vartheta}\eta_{B_{0}}}\sqrt{\frac{\alpha^{\star}s^{\star}k^{2}}{N}},

with probability at least
1-3\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\exp(-k_{m}s^{\star})
.

Step 3
.
Starting with
\mathbf{B}_{i}^{(0)}={\bf 0}
, after
L
iterations of Algorithm
2
, we have the following result by telescoping technique:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{(L)}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}\leq\pi_{0}^{L}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}+\frac{C_{\gamma}C_{\vartheta}}{1-\pi_{0}}\frac{\eta_{B_{0}}}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\|\mathbf{A}^{m(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}+\frac{C_{\delta,i}C_{\vartheta}\eta_{B_{0}}}{1-\pi_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{\alpha^{\star}s^{\star}k^{2}}{N}},

with probability at least
1-3\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\exp(-k_{m}s^{\star})
.

Lemma E.7
.

Consider the following objective function

\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i})=(\mathbf{B}_{i})=\frac{1}{2N}\bigg\|\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}-\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbf{B}_{ij}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{j}\bigg\|_{F}^{2},

where
\widehat{g}
is
L
-smooth and
\mu
-strongly convex with respect to
\mathbf{B}_{i}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times N}
for
i\in V
. Let one-step iterate of the update to be

\mathbf{B}_{i}^{+}=\mathcal{H}_{\alpha}\circ{\mathcal{T}}_{s}\left(\mathbf{B}_{i}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i})\right),

where
\eta\leq 1/L
. Let
\mathbf{Z}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times N}
for
i\in V
.
Define

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{K}}_{\cdot\backslash i}

\displaystyle=(\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}^{\top},\ldots,\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i-1}^{\top},\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}^{\top},\ldots,\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{p}^{\top})^{\top}(\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}^{\top},\ldots,\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i-1}^{\top},\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i+1}^{\top},\ldots,\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{p}^{\top})\in\mathbb{R}^{kp\times k(p-1)}

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}_{\cdot\backslash i}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top},\ldots,\mathbf{Z}_{i-1}^{\top},\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{\top},\ldots,\mathbf{Z}_{p}^{\top})^{\top}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top},\ldots,\mathbf{Z}_{i-1}^{\top},\mathbf{Z}_{i+1}^{\top},\ldots,\mathbf{Z}_{p}^{\top})\in\mathbb{R}^{kp\times k(p-1)}.

Then

\frac{1}{C_{\vartheta}}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{+}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}\leq\left(1-\eta\mu\right)\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}+\eta\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}_{\cdot\backslash i}-\mathbf{K}_{\cdot\backslash i})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}+\eta\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\mathbf{K}_{\cdot\backslash i}]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}

where
\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}
is defined in (
D.5
).

Proof.

First, we define

g(\mathbf{B}_{i})=\frac{1}{2N}\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{i}-\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbf{B}_{ij}\mathbf{Z}_{j}\bigg\|_{F}^{2}.

Apply Lemma
H.3
–
H.4
, we can write

\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{+}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}=\|\mathcal{H}_{\alpha}\circ{\mathcal{T}}_{s}\left(\mathbf{B}_{i}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i})\right)-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}\leq C_{\vartheta}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}-\eta[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F},

where
\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}
is defined in (
D.5
). Then, by (
D.14
), we can write

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{+}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\vartheta}\big\{\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}-\eta[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\eta[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}g(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}+\|[\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}g(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\big\},

where
C_{\vartheta}
is a constant depending on
\vartheta_{1}
and
\vartheta_{2}
defined in Section
C
.

Write

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}-

\displaystyle\eta[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}-\eta\left\{\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})\right\}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\VEC\left(\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\right)-\eta\VEC\left(\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})\right)\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\left\{\mathbf{I}_{k^{2}(p-1)}-\eta\int_{t=0}^{1}\nabla^{2}\widehat{g}(\VEC\left(t\mathbf{B}_{i}+(1-t)\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\right))dt\right\}\VEC\left(\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\right)\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|{\mathbf{I}_{k^{2}(p-1)}-\eta\int_{t=0}^{1}\nabla^{2}\widehat{g}(\VEC\left(t\mathbf{B}_{i}+(1-t)\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\right))dt}\bigg\|_{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\eta\mu\right)\|\mathbf{B}_{i}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}

(E.7)

Note that we can write
\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})=-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}_{\cdot\backslash i}
and
\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})=-\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\mathbf{K}_{\cdot\backslash i}
.
Then, we can write

\displaystyle\|\eta[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}\widehat{g}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})-\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}g(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}=\eta\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}_{\cdot\backslash i}-\mathbf{K}_{\cdot\backslash i})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F},

where
\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}=(-\mathbf{B}_{i1}^{\star}\cdots-\mathbf{B}_{ip}^{\star})\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times pk}
.
∎

Lemma E.8
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
5.2
and define

C_{\delta,i}=C_{2}\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{2})(1+\vartheta_{1})}\\
\times\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\rho_{x}^{1/2}\rho_{a}^{1/2}\{\rho_{b}^{1/2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{m,\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}^{1/2}+(\rho_{a}\rho_{b})^{1/2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{m,\mathbf{q}}\|_{2}^{1/2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}_{i}^{\mathbf{r}}\|_{2}^{1/2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}_{i}^{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}^{1/2}\},

where
C_{2}>0
is an universal constant, we have

\eta_{B_{0}}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}h_{i}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\leq C_{\delta,i}\eta_{B_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{\alpha^{\star}s^{\star}k^{2}}{N}},

with probability at least
1-\exp\{{-\alpha^{\star}ks^{\star}}\}
.

Proof of Lemma
E.8
.

We use similar argument of Lemma
D.4
and write that

[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}h_{i}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}=-\frac{1}{MN}\left[\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m,(n)}(\mathbf{y}_{\backslash\{i\}}^{m,(n)})^{\top}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})\right]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}},

where
\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m,(n)}
is defined in (
D.15
).

Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{1}({\mathcal{S}})

\displaystyle=\{\mathbf{u}:\text{supp}({\mathbf{u}})\subset\{{\mathcal{S}}_{\cdot,i},i=1,\ldots k(p-1)\},\|\mathbf{u}\|_{1}=1\},\;{\mathcal{S}}_{\cdot,i}=\{u:(u,i)\in{\mathcal{S}}\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{2}({\mathcal{S}})

\displaystyle=\{\mathbf{w}:\text{supp}(\mathbf{w})\subset\{{\mathcal{S}}_{i,\cdot},i=1,\ldots,k\},\|\mathbf{w}\|_{1}=1\},\;{\mathcal{S}}_{i,\cdot}=\{v;(i,v)\in{\mathcal{S}}\}.

For notation simplicity, we define
\mathcal{U}_{1}=\mathcal{U}_{1}(\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i})
and
\mathcal{U}_{2}=\mathcal{U}_{2}(\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i})
.
Let
\mathcal{N}_{1}
be the
1/8
-net of
\mathcal{U}_{1}
and
\mathcal{N}_{2}
be the
1/8
-net of
\mathcal{U}_{2}
.
Then, we can write

\displaystyle\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}h_{i}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{k}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}h_{i}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{k}\sup_{\mathbf{u}\in\mathcal{U}_{1}}\sup_{\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{U}_{2}}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\left[\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m,(n)}(\mathbf{y}_{\backslash\{i\}}^{m,(n)})^{\top}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})\right]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\mathbf{w}.

Apply Lemma
I.4
, we can upper bound the above display as

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{k}\sup_{\mathbf{u}\in\mathcal{N}_{1}}\sup_{\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{2}}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\left[\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{m,(n)}(\mathbf{y}_{\backslash\{i\}}^{m,(n)})^{\top}(\mathbf{I}_{p-1}\otimes{\mathbf{A}^{m\star\top}})\right]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\mathbf{w},

where the right hand side can be seen as sum of i.i.d. sub-exponential random variables with Orlicz norm upper bounded by
\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\rho_{x}^{1/2}\rho_{a}^{1/2}\{\rho_{b}^{1/2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{m,\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}^{1/2}+(\rho_{a}\rho_{b})^{1/2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{m,\mathbf{q}}\|_{2}^{1/2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}_{i}^{\mathbf{r}}\|_{2}^{1/2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}_{i}^{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}^{1/2}\}
, where a similar proof can be found in Lemma
D.4
.
Define

C_{\delta,i}=C_{2}\sqrt{(1+\vartheta_{2})(1+\vartheta_{1})}\\
\times\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\rho_{x}^{1/2}\rho_{a}^{1/2}\{\rho_{b}^{1/2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{m,\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}^{1/2}+(\rho_{a}\rho_{b})^{1/2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{m,\mathbf{q}}\|_{2}^{1/2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}_{i}^{\mathbf{r}}\|_{2}^{1/2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}_{i}^{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}^{1/2}\},

and
C_{2}>0
. Therefore, apply Bernstein’s inequality with
N=O(C_{\delta,i}^{2}\alpha^{\star}s^{\star}k)
and take the union bound over all
\mathcal{N}_{1}
with
|\mathcal{N}_{1}|\leq(17)^{(1+\vartheta_{1})\alpha^{\star}k}
and
\mathcal{N}_{2}
with
|\mathcal{N}_{2}|\leq(17)^{(1+\vartheta_{1})(1+\vartheta_{2})\alpha^{\star}ks^{\star}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{73,i}

\displaystyle=\eta_{B_{0}}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}h_{i}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{F}\leq\eta_{B_{0}}\sqrt{k}\|[\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{i}}h_{i}(\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star})]_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\|_{2}\leq C_{\delta,i}\eta_{B_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{\alpha^{\star}s^{\star}k^{2}}{N}},

with probability at least
1-\exp\{{-\alpha^{\star}ks^{\star}}\}\geq 1-\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\exp(-k_{m}s^{\star})
,

∎

Appendix F
Proof of Theorem
5.4

The main theorem combines results from Theorem
5.1
and Lemma
5.2
with result from Theorem
5.3
.

Step 1
: The first step is to show that
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}
computed from (
4.3
) for
m=1,\ldots,M
and
\mathbf{B}_{i}
outputed by Algorithm
2
for
i\in V
satisfy Assumption
8
.
Since
\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}\leq\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}
and
\sqrt{8k}|\!|\!|\mathscr{A}^{m}|\!|\!|^{2}|\!|\!|\mathscr{L}^{m,r}|\!|\!|
is a constant under the condition that
|\!|\!|\mathscr{L}^{m,r}|\!|\!|=o(1/\sqrt{k_{m}})
, Theorem
5.1
tells us that there exists a
N=O(\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}k_{m}+\log M)
, with
M=2
, such that
\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}\geq C_{A}\|\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}+\sqrt{8k}|\!|\!|\mathscr{A}^{m}|\!|\!|^{2}|\!|\!|\mathscr{L}^{m,r}|\!|\!|
with probability smaller than
2^{-1}\exp(-k_{1}-k_{2})
for each
m=1,2
.
Using the condition that
Taking the union bound, we have
\|{\mathbf{A}^{m}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}\geq C_{A}\|\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{2}
for
m=1,\ldots,M
with probability smaller than
\exp(-k_{1}-k_{2})
. We define this event as
\mathcal{E}_{A^{0}}^{c}
.

Similarly, since all norms in finite dimensional are equivalent, it suffices to show that
\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{(0)}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}\leq C_{B}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}
for
i\in V
. Under the condition
N=O(\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}s^{\star}k_{m}+\log M+\log p)
, apply Lemma
5.2
with the number of iteration
L_{0}=-C_{1}\log\{(k^{2}+\log s^{\star})\}/{N}\}
and Theorem
5.1
, we have
\|{\mathbf{B}_{i}^{(0)}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}}\|_{F}\geq C_{B}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}
with probability smaller than
3p^{-1}\delta_{0}
for each
i\in V
. Then, taking the union bound over
i\in V
, we have
\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{(0)}-\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}\geq C_{B}\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{F}
for
i\in V
with probability smaller than
3\delta_{0}
. We define such conditional event as
\mathcal{E}_{B^{0}\cap A^{0}}^{c}
.
Therefore, Assumption
8
holds with probability at least

1-\left\{\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{B^{0}}^{c})+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{A^{0}}^{c})\right\}\geq 1-\left\{\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{B^{0}\cap A^{0}}^{c})+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{A^{0}}^{c})\right\}\geq 1-4\delta_{0}.

Step 2
: Under Assumption
8
, we can apply Theorem
5.3
. The second part of the theorem can be shown by triangle inequality. Let
L
be any constant such that
L\geq(-\log(1-\pi)+2\log\Xi-2\log R_{0})/\log\pi
. This implies that for any
i\in V

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{B}_{i,j}^{(L)}-\mathbf{B}_{ij}^{\star}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\max_{m=1,\ldots,M}\|\mathbf{A}_{m}^{(L)}-\mathbf{A}^{m\star}\|_{F}^{2}+\max_{i\in V}\|\mathbf{B}_{i,j}^{(L)}-\mathbf{B}_{ij}^{\star}\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{2}{1-\pi}}\Xi\leq\frac{1}{2}\Lambda(k)

Suppose that
j\in\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}
, then

\|\mathbf{B}_{i,j}^{(L)}\|_{F}\geq\|\mathbf{B}_{ij}^{\star}\|_{F}-\|\mathbf{B}_{i,j}^{(L)}-\mathbf{B}_{ij}^{\star}\|_{F}\geq\Lambda(k)-\frac{1}{2}\Lambda(k)=\frac{1}{2}\Lambda(k),

with probability at least
1-10\delta_{0}
. Similarly, if
j\in{\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}}^{c}\backslash\{i\}
,

\|\mathbf{B}_{i,j}^{(L)}\|_{F}\leq\|\mathbf{B}_{i,j}^{(L)}-\mathbf{B}_{ij}^{\star}\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{2}\Lambda(k),

with probability at least
1-10\delta_{0}
. Therefore, under Assumption
4
, if we select edges with the threshold
(1/2)\Lambda(k)
, we are able to recover the edge set with probability at least
1-10\delta_{0}
.
Finally, marginalizing over the conditional probability that Assumption
8
holds, we have

\mathbb{P}(\widehat{\mathscr{N}}_{i}=\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star};1\leq i\leq p)\geq(1-10\delta_{0})(1-4\delta_{0})\geq 1-14\delta_{0}

Appendix G
Strong Convexity and Smoothness

In this section, we show the convexity coefficient and smoothness coefficient of
f(\cdot)
and
h_{i}(\cdot)
. Lemma
G.3
states that
f(\cdot,\mathbf{B}^{\star})
is strongly convex and smooth with respect to any
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}
for
m=1,\ldots,M
with high probability under proper sample size condition. Lemma
G.5
shows that under mild condition of
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}
for
m=1,\ldots,M
and sample size,
h_{i}(\cdot)
is strongly convex and smooth with respect
\mathbf{B}_{i}
with high probability. We being with reviewing the definition of
L
-smoothness and
\mu
-strong convexity.

Definition G.1
.

A function
f:\Domain(f)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is
L
-smooth if for any
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\Domain(f)
we have that

\left|f(\mathbf{y})-f(\mathbf{x})-\langle{\nabla f(\mathbf{x})},{\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{x}}\rangle\right|\leq\frac{L}{2}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

Definition G.2
.

A function
f:\Domain(f)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is
\mu
-strongly convex if for any
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\Domain(f)
we have that

f(\mathbf{y})\geq f(\mathbf{x})+\langle{\nabla f(\mathbf{x})},{\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{x}}\rangle+\frac{\mu}{2}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

Lemma G.3
.

Assume that Assumption
6
–
7
hold,

\|\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\star}\|_{r(k,0)}\leq s^{\star}
for
i\in V
and

\kappa_{m}=\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m})/\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m})
.
If
N=O(\kappa_{m}^{2}(k_{m}s^{\star}+\log M+\log p))
, then
f(\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}^{\star})

is
\{(s^{\star}+1)p\nu_{x}\nu_{b}\}/{2}
-strongly convex
and
\{3(s^{\star}+1)p\rho_{x}\rho_{b}\}/{2}
-smooth with respect to
{\mathbf{A}^{m}}

with probability at least
1-M^{-1}\exp\{-k_{m}(s^{\star}+1)\}
.

Proof of Lemma
G.3
.

Define the following two constants:

\displaystyle d_{1}

\displaystyle=({s^{\star}+1})\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}})\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}),

\displaystyle d_{2}

\displaystyle=({s^{\star}+1})\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}}\|_{2}.

By Lemma
G.4
, we have
f
be
d_{1}
-strongly convex. Under the condition
N=O(\kappa_{m}^{2}(k_{m}s^{\star}+\log M+\log p))
event
\mathcal{E}_{m}=\cap_{i=1}^{p}\mathcal{E}_{m,i}(\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\})
defined in (
D.21
) holds with probability at least
1-M^{-1}\exp\{-k_{m}(s^{\star}+1)\}
. Applying Lemma
I.1
yields
\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}})\geq 2^{-1}\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m}_{\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\},\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}})
for
i\in V
with probability at least
1-M^{-1}\exp\{-k_{m}(s^{\star}+1)\}
. Therefore we have

\displaystyle({s^{\star}+1})\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{m}_{\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}})\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{s^{\star}+1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m}_{\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\},\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}})\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star}),

with probability at least
1-M^{-1}\exp\{-k_{m}(s^{\star}+1)\}
.
Apply Assumption
6
–
7
, we can obtain a further lower bound and obtain
\mu
:

\frac{s^{\star}+1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sigma_{\min}(\bm{\Sigma}^{m}_{\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\},\mathscr{N}_{i}^{\star}\cup\{i\}})\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{\star})\geq\frac{(s^{\star}+1)p\nu_{x}\nu_{b}}{2}.
